## Unmodeled runtime tables

### ecomm_payment_intents

Holds a pending online checkout before gateway verification creates the bill and order. The stored request and server-calculated total let settlement resume safely and enforce idempotency.

Created by ecommerce-api startup DDL but missing from schema.zmodel; reconcile this schema drift before changing the table.

Created in: `ecommerce-api/api/app/tables.py`. References: `ecommerce-api/api/app/routes/checkout.py`, `ecommerce-api/api/app/routes/payment.py`, `ecommerce-api/api/app/tables.py`.

| Column | SQL declaration | Meaning |
|---|---|---|
| `id` | `text PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()::text` | Primary identifier of this pending payment attempt. |
| `created_at` | `timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()` | Time the payment intent was created. |
| `updated_at` | `timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now()` | Time the payment intent was last updated. |
| `status` | `text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING'` | Current state of the pending payment attempt. |
| `payment_method` | `text NOT NULL` | Online payment method selected for this checkout. |
| `grand_total` | `double precision NOT NULL` | Server-calculated amount expected from the gateway before order creation. |
| `request_payload` | `jsonb NOT NULL` | Saved checkout request used to create the order after payment verification. |
| `transaction_id` | `text` | Gateway or payment transaction reference attached to the intent. |
| `checkout_id` | `text` | Checkout created when the payment attempt is settled. |
| `company_id` | `text NOT NULL REFERENCES companies(id) ON DELETE CASCADE` | Company that owns the payment attempt; part of the tenant and idempotency scope. |
| `client_id` | `text NOT NULL REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE` | Customer who started the payment attempt; part of the idempotency scope. |
| `idempotency_key` | `text` | Client-supplied key used to prevent duplicate checkout attempts for the same company and customer. |

Exact SQL declaration, including every column and constraint:

```sql
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS ecomm_payment_intents (id              text PRIMARY KEY DEFAULT gen_random_uuid()::text,
          created_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
          updated_at      timestamptz NOT NULL DEFAULT now(),
          status          text NOT NULL DEFAULT 'PENDING',
          payment_method  text NOT NULL,
          grand_total     double precision NOT NULL,
          request_payload jsonb NOT NULL,
          transaction_id  text,
          checkout_id     text,
          company_id      text NOT NULL REFERENCES companies(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
          client_id       text NOT NULL REFERENCES clients(id) ON DELETE CASCADE,
          idempotency_key text);
```

### DistributorCompany

PostgreSQL table: `distributor_companies`. Source: `schema.zmodel:122`.

Purpose: Associates a distributor with a company.

Declared constraints and policies:

```prisma
@@id([distributorId, companyId])
@@allow('read,create, update, delete', auth() != null )
@@map('distributor_companies')
```

| Field | Kind/type | Required | Column/default | Relation | Meaning |
|---|---|---:|---|---|---|
| `distributorId` | scalar: String | yes | column `distributor_id` |  | Distributor associated with this purchase or payment. |
| `companyId` | scalar: String | yes | column `company_id` |  | Company that owns this DistributorCompany record; use it when scoping tenant data. |
| `distributor` | relation: Distributor | yes |  | distributorId → Distributor.id | Links this DistributorCompany record to Distributor through distributorId. |
| `company` | relation: Company | yes |  | companyId → Company.id | Links this DistributorCompany record to Company through companyId. |
| `distributorPayments` | relation: DistributorPayment[] | no |  |  | Links this DistributorCompany record to DistributorPayment. |

| `purchaseReturns` | relation: PurchaseReturn[] | no |  |  | Links this DistributorCompany record to PurchaseReturn. |
| `openingDue` | scalar: Float | no | column `opening_due`; default `0` |  | Amount owed to the distributor when this company-distributor relationship was created. |
| `openingDueDate` | scalar: DateTime | no | column `opening_due_date` |  | Date from which the opening distributor balance applies. |
| `distributorNumber` | scalar: Int | no | column `distributor_number` |  | Company-local identifier assigned to this distributor. |

| `bill` | relation: Bill | no |  | billId → Bill.id | Links this EcommCheckout record to Bill through billId. |
| `billId` | scalar: String | no | column `bill_id` |  | Bill associated with this EcommCheckout record. |
| `idempotencyKey` | scalar: String | no | column `idempotency_key` |  | custom-api's double-submit guard: the storefront sends a key per checkout attempt and the unique index below is what actually stops a double-click becoming two orders (an application-level check can be raced). storetools never writes this, but it MUST stay declared here — the column only survives a `zenstack generate` + `prisma db push` if the zmodel knows about it, and dropping it breaks storefront checkout outright. |
| `order` | relation: EcommOrder | no |  |  | Links this EcommCheckout record to EcommOrder. |

Code references (direct text/accessor matches): `storetools/server/utils/aiUsage.ts`, `storetools/server/utils/storefrontAgent.ts`

Declared constraints and policies:

```prisma
@@unique([source, interactionId])
@@index([companyId, createdAt(sort: Desc)], map: "ai_usage_events_company_created_idx")
@@allow('read', auth() != null)
@@map("ai_usage_events")
```

| Field | Kind/type | Required | Column/default | Relation | Meaning |
|---|---|---:|---|---|---|
| `id` | scalar: String | yes | default `dbgenerated("gen_random_uuid()::text")` |  | Primary key identifying this AiUsageEvent record. |
| `source` | scalar: String | yes | default `"storefront_agent"` |  | Origin of this event or record. |
| `conversationId` | scalar: String | no | column `conversation_id` |  | Conversation associated with this AiUsageEvent record. |
| `interactionId` | scalar: String | yes | column `interaction_id` |  | Agent interaction associated with this AiUsageEvent record. |
| `model` | scalar: String | no |  |  | AI model selected for this operation. |
| `status` | scalar: String | no |  |  | Current workflow state of this AiUsageEvent record. |
| `inputTokens` | scalar: BigInt | yes | column `input_tokens`; default `0` |  | Number of model input tokens recorded for this operation. |
| `outputTokens` | scalar: BigInt | yes | column `output_tokens`; default `0` |  | Number of model output tokens recorded for this operation. |
| `thoughtsTokens` | scalar: BigInt | yes | column `thoughts_tokens`; default `0` |  | Reasoning tokens reported for this AI interaction. |
| `cachedTokens` | scalar: BigInt | yes | column `cached_tokens`; default `0` |  | Input tokens served from provider cache. |
| `totalTokens` | scalar: BigInt | yes | column `total_tokens`; default `0` |  | Total tokens reported for this AI interaction. |
| `createdAt` | scalar: DateTime | yes | column `created_at`; default `now()` |  | Time this AiUsageEvent record was created. |
| `updatedAt` | scalar: DateTime | yes | column `updated_at`; default `now()` |  | Time this AiUsageEvent record was last updated. |
| `company` | relation: Company | yes |  | companyId → Company.id | Links this AiUsageEvent record to Company through companyId. |
| `companyId` | scalar: String | yes | column `company_id` |  | Company that owns this AiUsageEvent record; use it when scoping tenant data. |
